Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in complexity theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 217 and the invariant I(A,B) = 573 mod 5.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 217 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the complexity structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 573. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 5. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.

This completes the proof. QED.
